# Reviewed build progress, 2026-10-04

This addendum updates the dated twenty-product work inventory after checkpoint 113. It retains all 344 original requirements and 61 work groups, every original acceptance boundary, and the later Proj/Book/Town/Desk vision deltas. Counts are evidence states, never completion percentages. No new requirement is marked verified.

Checkpoint 122 is an independently approved interim delivery, not product completion. Mission-study recovery, Find/MoonChat artifact-review r2, the separate Flow unresolved-attempt readiness fix and Robo digital-trial receipts are later independently reviewed source milestones and must not be represented as contents of checkpoint 122.

## Estimate boundary

The original conditional planning range is retained from the 2026-10-04 14:29 assessment. Completed subwork is listed separately; remaining effort has not been recalculated. This range is not a precise current remaining estimate or a delivery promise. All group/milestone bounds, effort-breakdown arithmetic, links, dependencies and confidence values remain unchanged. The earlier conditional 36–63 engineer-day release planning budget also has not been recalculated and is not a current remaining-work forecast.

## Evidence and source boundary

Completed means only the explicit bounded local acceptance slice below. Independent postchecks establish that reviewed patches were promoted to recovered cloud source, not deployment, live provider/customer acceptance, installed desktop quality or latest upstream/Mac parity. Earlier frozen reports may correctly say pending promotion; their separate immutable postcheck is the later evidence. MoonChat integration changes are in Find and use the unchanged pinned MoonChat package. Flow's company bridge changes are in MoonProj and originally exercised historical Flow source; the later Flow readiness fix is a separate source promotion and does not rewrite the bridge's historical evidence.

New totals are 178 partial, 97 unverified, 68 planned and the same one narrow verified MOONMOLD-B05. Counts describe evidence states, not completion percentages. Remaining effort has not been recalculated; original conditional budgets are not a current remaining-work forecast.

## Source, compatibility and verification boundaries

The feature reviews establish only their named source/reducer/transport/native-fixture slices. No browser pixels, real file-picker/focus/keyboard, live provider/company database, external customer/cash, deployment or installed Mac qualification is inferred. Earlier frozen feature reports can say pending promotion; their separate exact postchecks establish the later source state. Fort forwarding changed Desk/Claw only, carries two fixed configuration references with inherit_env=false and leaves Bookkeeper/Flow stubs unresolved.

Book preserves complete legacy wire inputs, canonical encoder bytes and persisted journal identities. It does not preserve unchanged native source construction: four String fields in each of ProductOutcomeSubmission and ProductOutcomeClosureRequest become String?, so direct native callers must handle Some/None. All-four capability omission requires every supplied gap explicitly false/zero; partial/invalid omission refuses. The existing founder continuation stops at assessment, while general no-gap closure emits no capability proposal or egress. Representation validation does not verify real observations.

This inventory update itself uses small Python/JSON/source checks and an exact Python translation of the unchanged importer. The translation must reproduce r6 bytes before generating r7 and validate all authored PRD hashes/body text. The original MoonBit importer/compiler/parser tests were not executed for r7; no build is claimed. The r6 compiler checks remain historical evidence only.

# Immutable human-revision progress, r8, 2026-10-05

R8 is a separate annotation-only data/document candidate based on the exact post-human-revision current source and unchanged r7 inventory. The 19-path Desk and 21-path Proj source pair has an independent final promotion postcheck. This update makes no runtime, authored PRD, status, estimate, publication or installed-host change.

The same 20 products, 344 requirements (242 functional and 102 quality), 61 groups and every requirement/group status remain unchanged: 178 partial, 97 unverified, 68 planned and the same one narrow verified MOONMOLD-B05. Exactly eight existing rows and four groups receive additive notes; no status, original requirement, acceptance/history, milestone, estimate/arithmetic/confidence, membership or ambition changes. Counts are evidence states, not completion percentages. Remaining effort has not been recalculated and no delivery date is forecast. Complete r7 and earlier progress records remain dated history.

## Boundaries that remain explicit

The first slice retains one verified UTF-8 output, at most 256 KiB, as an immutable human revision. It never writes the live agent/workspace file. The base is current-at-edit-start, not necessarily originally reviewed bytes. Saving/returning is separate from explicit human_revision source selection, Report and Accept. Selection/reporting does not cancel or complete native execution; existing explicit Accept can mark the task done while native execution continues. Later observations retain the human report. Accepted/done tasks remain inspection-only.

Desk recovery after reload/lost response still requires native session/Book/output identity readback, including archive/ambiguity refusal. Exact draft/operation identity remains visible as confirmation pending while unavailable; local intent cannot claim saved. Definitive conflicts are durable/re-flushed, uncertain writes remain uncertain, and rebase uses a new identity. Quota failure retains the in-memory draft and refuses dispatch.

MOONPROJ-N01 retains its exact authored canonical-task-ID-only task-URL acceptance. The new nonsecret human_revision_id navigation hint does not fulfill that narrower criterion; it remains unresolved, with no silent requirement amendment. Canonical readback and actor/source fencing are bounded evidence only.

Existing Proj outcome/lesson projections preserve human source and manifest digest without a fabricated native completion receipt. This is pure projection composition, not new MoonBook import, review, adoption, learning activation or a portable copy of content.txt. R7 Book evidence and its native String-to-String? migration retain their original scope.

The evidence is bounded exact-source native drivers, controlled gateway/service/SQL fixtures and compiled UI tests, not mounted/live acceptance. Real sockets and signature exchange, PostgreSQL-enforced transactions, full async report INSERT execution, full host/gateway/service executable linking, mounted-browser behavior, provider execution and installed Mac behavior remain unqualified. Live agent bytes are not written by this feature; 256 KiB UTF-8 is the first-slice bound, and Desk lost-response/reload recovery retains the native identity gate. No customer/cash acceptance or Book activation is established.

## Evidence execution boundary

The independent feature review reused existing compiled drivers and reran Desk backend 16/16, Proj gateway 8/8, Proj service 10/10, Desk direct UI 9/9 and Proj targeted UI 14/14. Broader author totals (Desk 36 focused and 701 full-main JS; Proj 304 frontend) were reviewed from logs, not all independently rerun. Earlier full Desk native driver attempts failed before tests; their failures are not passing evidence. SQL fixtures use exact production selection/guard/projection logic but do not execute live database transactions or the complete async report INSERT.

This inventory update itself runs only small Python/JSON/hash checks and sparse patch replay. The unchanged importer is translated in Python and must reproduce exact r7 baseline bytes before validating r8 output. The original MoonBit importer/compiler/parser and product suites are not executed here; no new compiler or runtime pass is claimed.

# Reviewed guidance and first-customer proposal progress (r9, 2026-10-05)

Record the independently reviewed and promoted Book guidance-to-first-native-request and canonical task-linked first-customer proposal slices. Annotate five existing Proj/Book requirements and four existing groups; only MOONPROJ-R02 advances from planned to partial. Preserve every authored criterion, historical record, scope, group partition and conditional estimate. No full-criterion verification, live PostgreSQL/browser/provider, customer outcome or measured learning is inferred.

## Scope and counts

All 344 authored requirements (242 functional, 102 quality), 61 groups and 20 products remain. The only status change is MOONPROJ-R02: planned to partial. Totals are 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified; no new requirement or group is verified and every group status is unchanged. Counts describe evidence scope, never completion percentages. Every statement, acceptance criterion, historical record, requirement/group identity and membership, dependency, non-goal, philosophy and conditional estimate is preserved. Remaining effort is not recalculated. The base as_of date remains 2026-10-04; this addendum and assessment revision record the later evidence cutoff.

The complete r3-r8 record remains dated history. R9 supersedes only the explicitly bounded current-gap/next-action wording below. Historical feature reports marked pending promotion are qualified by the separate later independent postchecks; this inventory candidate itself remains unpromoted pending review.

## Evidence and behavior boundaries

R9 evidence is production UI serialization/reconciliation with controlled browser objects plus exact-source native command/request/SQL/readback fixtures. No real PostgreSQL transaction/lock/crash enforcement, sockets/signatures, mounted-browser storage/download/popup/accessibility, provider behavior or installed-Mac operation is established. No customer demand, signed customer order, delivery acceptance, cash, model adherence, capability activation or measured learning follows. Authored criteria and original conditional estimates remain unchanged.

Guidance is optional, tab-local before Start and excluded from URLs and general draft storage/exports. An explicit destination confirmation and fresh journal selection precede return; private guidance, applicability and reviewer details stay out of ordinary public execution readback. The selected observation is capped at 32 KiB UTF-8 and the complete reservation at 128 KiB; oversize rejects rather than truncates. No-selection Start and the separate accepted-outcome/minimal saved-lesson return stay unchanged. Identical retries retain original request bytes; changed advice cannot replace a reservation. Capture is an unsigned dated observation with no freshness TTL, not model adherence or factual verification.

Proposal recovery is actor-scoped in the current browser; this is not a new claim that the original whole-draft privacy criterion is satisfied. Exports exclude actor/authority/token/operation/native-execution identities. New canonical updates require creator ownership, an ordinary command-owned pending task and no execution/report/delivery lineage. Exact operation replay remains possible after later revision/state changes. Unknown values and demand-unproven remain explicit; proposed customer criteria do not replace task acceptance. New planning from executing/accepted work is a separate empty local draft, not a mutation or task creation. Closing/revising an offer never implies external acceptance or cash.

## Shared qualification boundary

R10 qualification consists of production reducers/transports/rendered HTML and exact-source gateway/native command/SQL/readback fixtures. No actual PostgreSQL transaction/lock/crash enforcement, signed sockets/live service, mounted-browser storage/accessibility behavior, provider or installed-Mac operation is established. Today is one explicitly selected actual company project's observed queue, not a company-wide catalog or a default first-project selection. Products remains the boot default. Working content is actor-scoped tab session storage, not guaranteed after tab closure; failed storage removal is reported. # Accepted human-result portability progress (r11, 2026-10-05)

R11 adds bounded source/test observations to seven existing Proj/Desk/Book requirement rows and six existing groups. All 344 requirements (242 functional, 102 quality), 61 groups and 20 products remain. Every requirement/group status is unchanged: 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified. Counts are evidence states, never completion percentages. All authored criteria/history, source PRDs, scope, membership, dependencies, philosophy/non-goals, milestone links and conditional estimates remain intact. Remaining effort is not recalculated. The other 17 product records, including Town and Fish, are unchanged.

The base as_of date remains 2026-10-04; this dated revision records the 2026-10-05T14:56:59Z evidence cutoff. All r3-r10 document bytes remain as history. R11 adds only the explicitly bounded carrier/integrity observations below, not new full-criterion fulfillment.

## Source and meaning boundaries

The actual chain is Proj read-only accepted-human-result export → Desk explicit file selection/preview and explicit existing Prepare → unchanged Book package API → existing Town exact-byte transfer → unchanged Book exact-import API. This adds Proj/Desk source code and one Book journey test. Town production transfer, Book production code/public APIs, the prior outcome/lesson/guidance contracts and acceptance authority are unchanged. No Town result viewer, new ledger/runner, automatic repack or learning review is added.

The Proj export reads an accepted task/report/receipts and exact pinned retained human revision/body, not the mutable original output. Empty text is a valid zero-byte body; strict UTF-8/NUL and the existing 262,144-byte limit remain enforced. The downloaded source JSON remains byte-for-byte unchanged. Its body and original-file SHA-256 verify actual bytes; revision_snapshot_digest hashes deterministic serialization of the accepted parsed manifest, not original lexical whitespace. Export metadata has no workspace/output path. An already accepted revision requires no new native continuation attachment or runtime command.

Desk labels the imported file an unsigned captured source observation. Choosing a file alone does not prepare a package or authenticate a live Proj relationship. Explicit Prepare stages original source.json and decoded content.txt as two members of one inert selected output. Backend checks exact schema/duplicates, Base64, UTF-8/NUL, size/digests and preparation identity. Existing manual selections and internal-path rejection remain. The new portable-Book carrier is not the separate authored HTML Work Package acceptance or a comprehensive privacy qualification of all book files/credentials/outside inputs.

Existing ordinary artifact review_status accepted denotes package inclusion, not source authenticity, a Bookkeeper outcome, named lesson approval or reusable-rule acceptance. Imported byte integrity cannot supply live company/claim/acceptance authenticity, customer/cash evidence, model behavior, capability activation or measured learning.

Qualification uses synthetic offline exact production functions, controlled company-upstream boundaries, reducers/rendered HTML and Node WebCrypto. It does not exercise live signed company service, real sockets/sessions, mounted browser file picker/storage/accessibility, Desk's installed-compatible-host subprocess, providers or the user's Mac. Existing broader PRD gates remain open.

# Town Imported outputs and timeout-test progress (r12, 2026-10-05)

R12 adds source/test notes to four existing Town requirements and two existing groups. No requirement or group status changes. Totals remain 344 requirements (242 functional, 102 quality), 61 groups and 20 products: 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified. Counts are evidence states, not completion percentages. Criteria/history, scope, membership, dependencies, philosophy/non-goals, milestone links and every conditional estimate/arithmetic remain exact. Remaining effort was not recalculated. The other 19 product records remain unchanged.

The original as_of stays 2026-10-04. R12 uses the later evidence cutoff 2026-10-05T17:03:48Z. All r3-r11 document bytes remain dated history. In particular, r11's no-viewer and unresolved-timeout statements describe that earlier portability slice/cutoff. The separately reviewed changes below add a viewer and correct the test; they do not expand or rewrite earlier qualification or failed logs.

## Read-only Imported outputs scope

The normal Town toolbar opens a drawer selecting an existing exact local imported Book, declared output and included member. A server-owned Book handle plus package/manifest/artifact/member path/size/digest pins bind the selection. The native host hashes/serves one held member buffer and rechecks provenance after reading. The UI verifies those actual bytes with shared MoonBit SHA-256 before a still-current selection can emit a save command using that same buffer.

Strict UTF-8 appears as escaped inert text; opaque formats are not rendered as active content. Binary/SVG/HTML can be downloaded as inert exact bytes. Empty, missing, changed, oversized, static-host-unavailable and unsupported ordinary-import states remain explicit. Reference-only outputs are displayed without fetching their references. Ordinary Desk imports without the required exact local import receipt are clearly unsupported. Source agent, local Book and folder identities remain distinct.

GET/HEAD routes remain behind the existing access gate and precede supervisor construction. This adds no package transfer/import, supplied application execution, work dispatch, permission change, Book truth mutation or lesson promotion. Retained local import provenance is not fresh publisher-signature or live-source verification. Existing pre/open/post filesystem checks and byte digests do not guarantee atomic defense against hostile concurrent directory renames.

Direct Town and the existing Desk packaged-app prefix are exercised. Desk's unchanged proxy can omit optional Content-Length/X-Artifact-Digest; wrong present values still reject. Mandatory status/type/attachment/nosniff, stream bounds, actual byte count and pinned SHA remain enforced. No WebCrypto secure-context dependency or new dependency version is introduced. Competing surface/navigation actions close/invalidate the drawer; stale bodies cannot display or create a save effect.

Bounds remain explicit: 64 KiB receipt/Book metadata, 16 MiB manifest/member, 4 MiB catalog response and 4 MiB inert text preview. A readable artifact does not establish the original configured goal/run/review journey or full supervisor/civic intervention criteria. MOONTOWN-B05 therefore stays unverified; N02 stays planned.

## Final viewer evidence and limits

R3 independent review passed 23/23 native, 5/5 shared JS, 1/1 real Sender test covering four HEAD→GET fixture streams, 41/41 source-exact UI feature/adjacent tests, 6/6 extracted browser-glue tests, 17/17 prior surface controls, 3/3 prior proxy controls and 4/4 actual-response decoder/render checks. The direct/Desk-proxy matrix completes 60 explicitly awaited cases (17 accepted, 43 rejected); success requires a current save effect and none after reverse navigation. Final combined replay reports 63/63 with the complete 60-case marker. These scopes overlap and must not be summed as distinct behaviors.

Four actual accepted-human responses preserve earlier package/manifest/artifact/member identities and exact Unicode/BOM/CRLF/markup, empty, maximum accepted-human 256-KiB and later-version bodies. All 285 retained imported-member hashes and four original packages were checked; source reads did not mutate recipients/fixtures. Final source promotion/postcheck confirms 36 paths (25 new, 11 replaced) and the exact 3,173-file Town source/metadata, retaining the separately promoted timeout test.

This is source-exact/native/piped-HTTP/Node-response/rendered-HTML evidence, not a mounted browser, real browser download, screenshot, live Desk/Town service/socket host, full desktop server executable build, provider or Mac qualification. Native checks use recovered async 0.19.4; they do not establish standalone declared 0.16.6 or whole-root dependency/build compatibility. R1/R2 failures and fixture adaptations remain retained; final R3 receipts supersede their unresolved findings only within this bounded slice.

## Separately verified timeout-test correction

The single-file change is src/town_portable_http/framing_wbtest.mbt. Controlled unchanged production functions prove the eager MemoryReader's 60 ms producer delay can finish during authorization/recovery before the 20 ms body-read idle timer is armed; a resulting 200 is legitimate. Old unrelated successful operations also shared 100 ms total/20 ms I/O budgets. This is a demonstrated test setup race, not evidence requiring a production timeout change.

The replacement test-only StalledReader waits on an unreleased semaphore only after actual read entry, records cancellation and requires one read, exact HTTP 408, exact timeout payload and closed connection. A timeout-specific clone retains the original 20 ms idle deadline; normal-budget success checks preserve same-store recovery, exact binary download and strict control assertions. No longer producer sleep, retry, skip, production code/API/dependency/timeout or clock change is introduced. The 120-second bounded run cannot pass by waiting for the five-minute total watchdog; response status alone would not distinguish the two timers.

Two candidate full runs and the independent copied-harness run report 16/16 framework cases. Fifteen bodies actually execute: the existing actual-byte package test is opt-in and returns without its body when MOONTOWN_PACKAGE_ACCEPTANCE_DIR is absent. Those runs are not fresh package-transfer qualification. The previously enabled accepted-human package proof stays separate.

The one-file promotion and independent full-source postcheck preserve 3,147 other original files, metadata, API and all three historical logs. The later viewer postcheck still verifies corrected test SHA-256 e7213c6d500bb97f0af0deae6ffe0c198711f6d7280246193be6274273cccedd. Earlier 15/16 and isolated-pass evidence is not erased. This correction improves regression reliability; it does not satisfy production authentication, backup/restore, forced-restart supervision or multi-day service acceptance, so N04 remains unverified.

## Inventory-update validation boundary

R12 separately uses the unchanged original importer to reproduce r11 exactly, then generate/check r12 against all original authored definitions and packaged DOCX criteria. Narrow inventory parser/check/interface/format, asset/invariant and sparse replay checks do not rerun the viewer/timeout product suites or a broad native server build. Complete unchanged-source guards cover MoonProj 1,363 plus Town 3,173 files (4,536 total) under each source's declared exclusions. No further feature audit, primary promotion, provider, publication or Mac work is part of this candidate.


## moontown

R12 bounded progress: the normal toolbar now opens a read-only Imported outputs drawer for an existing exact local Book import, a declared output and an included member. A server-owned Book handle plus package/manifest/artifact/path/size/digest pins bind selection. Strict bounded UTF-8 is displayed as escaped text; opaque formats remain downloadable as inert exact bytes. Reference-only items are not fetched. Empty, absent, changed, unsupported ordinary import, static-host and size-limit cases remain explicit. Direct and existing Desk packaged-app paths verify the held body before a current save effect; navigation invalidates stale display/download work. Ordinary Desk imports without the required exact local import receipt are clearly unsupported. This adds a viewer after r11's separately bounded portability slice without expanding that earlier qualification. Separately, a one-file idle-timeout regression repair is independently verified/promoted: the original eager-producer race was reproduced, and 16 reported framework cases pass with 15 active bodies because an existing actual-byte test is opt-in. Earlier 15/16/isolated-pass history remains unchanged. Broader configured goal/run/review, supervisor intervention, authority-injection acceptance, live authentication/backup/restore/soak, browser/server build and measured coordination outcomes remain open.

## Bounded round-two feature progress (r13, 2026-10-06)

This revision records four narrow delivered cloud-source slices: MoonProj Across projects Today, MoonDesk human-revision comparison, Desk's optional exact original-Book-bound import/read-only Town handoff, and MoonFind's evidence-choice worksheet. The authored PRDs are unchanged. These are additions to the existing remaining-work assessment, not new product rows, formal tasks or a claim that their broader acceptance criteria are complete.

All twelve affected requirement statuses remain unchanged after checking the actual prior rows. Find R02 was already partial and now has more bounded implementation evidence. Proj N04 and Desk N05 require mounted keyboard/accessibility journeys; Town B05 requires live supervisor intervention and stale frontend/backend authority behavior. Those three stay unverified. The other nine were already partial and retain broader incomplete criteria. Counts remain 344 requirements (179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned, 1 verified), 61 groups and 20 products; counts are evidence categories, not completion percentages. Every statement, acceptance criterion, historical evidence/status, dependency, membership, estimate and confidence is retained. The original planning estimates have not been recalculated.

### Qualification boundary

Current evidence is cloud source, compiled reducers/rendered HTML, intercepted request effects and isolated real Book CLI/local-filesystem fixtures. Actual mounted-browser keyboard/assistive-technology journeys, signed live company/PostgreSQL, configured complete desktop host, provider, installed Mac, customer/cash evidence, full cross-machine lifecycle and measured learning remain open. Package/member pins establish byte correspondence, not source truth or review/lesson promotion. No comparison/import/read/export action grants execution or acceptance authority.

The new evidence references and hashes below distinguish source identity from test scope. Feature-suite counts overlap and must not be summed into portfolio acceptance. The inventory's own deterministic importer/parser/count tests are separate from feature qualification. Historical source-only records and earlier failures remain retained in the preceding sections.

### Bounded final checks

- Proj Today: independent affected OPC suite 401/401, comprising the author's 399 cases plus two review-only probes, and release JS build. The probes cover interleaved scoped retries, admission and stale replies. This is current-batch fan-out of three, not a global limit on still-finishing invalidated transports
- Desk human-revision comparison: separate affected UI suite 716/716 plus one exhaustive review-only LCS probe. That standalone result is retained as earlier qualification, not added to the combined suite
- Combined Desk comparison and optional exact import: affected UI 720/720 directly in the candidate with original manifests, release JS build and full product native check; native affected import/trusted-host suite 48/48. The combined candidate's own new native response also passes the separate frontend-consumer test 1/1. The real Book CLI ordinary/exact fixtures retain a 475,464-byte synthetic package with 57 members and an included Unicode human output. These are host-core/local filesystem calls; no HTTP listener or native app is implied
- Town: existing current published-main reader discovers retained exact-import fixtures as ready, returns the exact 45-byte output and digest, renders an inert preview and answers HEAD with an empty body. An initial leg used the earlier same-source retained native-pair fixture; the final combined leg separately consumes the combined Desk run's own exact-import output. The two physical disposable fixture paths and both receipts are preserved. Neither is a browser/socket round trip
- Find: final independent affected UI suite 98/98, separate restored-backup option-ID probe 1/1, and release JS build. A reviewer-found restored-ID edge case was fixed and rechecked. The separately identified existing-main compile repair is prerequisite build restoration, not worksheet feature completion
- Inventory: unchanged deterministic importer first reproduces exact r12, then generates and checks r13 against every authored JSON hash and packaged PRD body. Production parser checks pass on native/JS; five tests per target include the actual 344-row revised corpus. Existing asset verifier passes. Parser interface/production source and packaged PRD/catalog bytes are unchanged


### moonproj

R13: The explicit Across projects Today scope aggregates only successfully read projects from the authenticated current project list, retains project attribution, owner/deadline/blocker observations, exact task opening and retryable partial failures. Coverage is successful reads over listed projects, not all-company completeness. Three reads are admitted per current batch; invalidated old transports can finish but cannot settle newer state. Live signed service, mounted keyboard/accessibility, cross-product intake and the full reviewed-learning morning/evening loop remain open.

### moondesk

R13: Human revisions now have read-only before/after and conflict comparisons, explicit bounded/coarse diff labels and exact full-text downloads; the immutable revision/save/recovery protocol is retained. Separately, optional exact original Town Book binding accompanies the existing package import and enables a pinned read-only imported-output handoff. Ordinary import stays available without that receipt. Live desktop/browser/sidecar operation, Office fidelity, full producer review return, execution authority and accessibility journeys remain open.

### moonbook

R13: The unchanged current Book CLI is exercised by Desk's optional exact-bound import, verify and recovery with a real local package. This is an additional bounded consumer/receipt handoff, not a Book implementation change, proof of source truth, complete upgrade/rollback, cross-machine lifecycle or runtime activation.

### moontown

R13: The existing imported-output reader can consume an exact receipt from Desk's optional original-Book-bound import. This narrows r12's ordinary-Desk-import gap only for the explicit exact-binding path; imports without an exact receipt remain unsupported. Read-only fixture discovery and held-byte response do not qualify mounted supervisor intervention, live service authority or business outcomes.

### moonfind

R13: A user-authored evidence-choice worksheet retains two to four options and up to six criteria alongside strongest-source/claim references, contradictions, unknowns, cost of error and a smallest falsifying experiment. Book/run-scoped browser-local recovery and Markdown/JSON briefs retain evidence snapshots and explicit historical/unknown states. No automatic ranking, model-verified advice, company decision, customer-demand proof or acceptance is inferred. Mounted browser/download/accessibility, live research providers and formal Proj integration remain open. A separate narrow pre-existing main-branch compile repair is identified independently from the worksheet feature.

Evidence cutoff UTC: 2026-10-06T04:21:02Z. Source-assessment and frozen three-file patch identities are recorded separately in the r13 validation receipt. Fish, MoonProp and Mooncake are unchanged/excluded. No baseline, feature candidate, published repository or Mac was changed by the inventory update.

## Bounded round-three feature progress (r14, 2026-10-06)

This revision records four narrow cloud-source features against the exact delivered round-two trees: MoonFind decision JSON into MoonProj local work, MoonFlow owner actions in CLI and MoonDesk, MoonCast named multi-cut delivery, and MoonMold deterministic-reference cylinders. No authored PRD, catalog row or production inventory parser/API is changed by this assessment patch.

Thirteen existing requirements and nine existing groups receive additive evidence/next-action notes. All selected requirements were already partial and remain partial; every other status is unchanged. Counts remain 344 requirements (179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned, 1 verified), 61 groups and 20 products. Every original ID, statement, acceptance criterion, historical evidence/status, dependency, membership, conditional estimate and confidence is retained. Counts describe evidence categories, not completion percentages, and original planning estimates have not been recalculated.

The map deliberately avoids neighboring maturity claims: Find R01 is the opposite formal commitment-to-research contract, not this reverse local draft consumer; Cast N02 requires exact approved master-input/rebuild qualification, not merely new cut/SRT output; Flow reviewer understanding and Proj/Desk mounted accessibility remain open. Existing accepted work is not customer acceptance or payment. Mold's previously recorded box-only boundary is superseded only for the new deterministic mock/reference exporter, not Blender, CAD or physical engineering.

### Qualification boundary

Evidence comprises frozen cloud source, compiled reducers/rendered HTML, exact actual producer-consumer fixtures, isolated real CLI histories, synthetic FFmpeg cut/SRT/package bytes and deterministic mock geometry packages. These do not establish mounted-browser/file-picker/storage/accessibility journeys, live signed company/PostgreSQL, configured providers, installed Mac, actual client/customer acceptance, cash, measured learning, full restart/backup/recovery, Blender or physical authority.

The unchanged importer first reproduces the exact r13 corpus, then generates and checks r14 against original authored hashes and packaged PRD body extraction. Parser/native/JS tests, exact scope/history/estimate checks and the existing product asset verifier qualify the assessment only. Feature-suite counts overlap and are not added into portfolio acceptance. Historical evidence and known failures remain retained below and in earlier dated revisions.


### Bounded final checks

The authoritative Proj source/review is v2: preview leads to one explicit Prepare editable draft action, with source/freshness notices retained and no separate acknowledgment gate. Four UI/docs/test paths changed from v1; the pure consumer and formal signed-command path are unchanged. The fresh v2 full UI/build evidence supersedes the earlier UI qualification for the delivered source.

- Research handoff: independent Proj OPC 410/410, pure consumer 7/7 on each JS and native, and JS release build pass. Unchanged Find runs 98 existing UI cases plus one reviewer-only actual exporter case, 99/99 total; its current/historical/undecided outputs are the shipped consumer fixtures. Interruption checks are reducer-injected generations plus FFI source review, not an executed mounted or controlled-DOM picker journey
- Flow/Desk: final exact-graph Flow root/CLI native aggregate 66/66, stripped debug CLI build, and seven real CLI event-history scenarios pass. Bare/status-member JSON and text agree without event-file changes. The actual shipped Desk UI workspace passes 724/724 and its MoonBit JS release build; the worker's scoped native host freshness tests pass 2/2. No whole Desk native-suite, mounted browser or live orchestration pass is claimed
- Cast: exact-graph UI 99/99 and JS release build, independent native caption/execution 4/4 and worker native Studio-host aggregate 41/41 pass. Fresh synthetic outputs are separate three-second landscape and four-second portrait cuts with audio; independent inspection verifies exact half-open SRT intersections, Unicode/multiline text, source variants, all 15 ZIP members and unchanged original master/SRT/prior archive hashes. Optional standalone native Studio release optimization was deliberately stopped with exit 130 and produced no executable; this remains stopped/incomplete, separate from passing native checks and tests
- Mold: corrected frozen source passes scoped Node 43/43, app UI 35/35 with JS release build, and core 12/12 on each wasm, wasm-gc, JS and native. Independent actual mixed nine-sided cylinder/box, 16-sided primary cylinder, replaced-cylinder and reset-after-cylinder packages each have all eight member hashes/lengths checked. Semantic replay, OBJ geometry/units/indexing/outward winding/edge balance/signed volume/bounds are inspected. Cylinder-containing history retains the primitive contract after replacement/reset; genuinely cylinder-free legacy packages retain exact bytes. Worker exact-graph compatibility passes 3/3; native runtime 2/2 uses a verification-copy test-root literal relocation only, not an exact-source test-fixture pass. Prior SVGs were statically rasterized and visually inspected without a browser

### Dependency and retained-failure boundaries

Final feature qualification uses the actual delivered dependency graphs and unchanged module/workspace metadata. Proj resolves x 0.4.50, Rabbita 0.12.4, Lepusa 0.1.4, MoonLib 0.1.19 and transitive async 0.19.4. Flow uses exact x 0.4.40/async 0.19.4/MoonLib 0.1.22. Desk uses its existing UI workspace and full parent source; its transitive minimum-version resolution is documented in the final review. Cast root uses x 0.4.50 and UI exact x 0.4.40. Mold uses its published x 0.4.45 ordinary root graph and Rabbita 0.12.4. Earlier thin Desk or substituted-cache runs are retained setup history and are superseded for final qualification; no hidden workspace/version override is a delivered requirement.

The broad historical Mold Node run is not all-green: 61 passes, five environment/fixture failures and two skips are retained. Its missing Blender, Mac-only path and external-image assumptions are not fixed or qualified by the scoped geometry work. Earlier dependency-wide UI/workspace runs included unrelated Rabbita DOM/URL cases lacking a browser; final app-scoped checks do not retroactively make those runs green. An earlier native runtime path failure is separate from the explicitly relocated verification-copy test. Existing compiler/dependency warnings remain. No Vite distribution, mounted browser, installed Mac, configured provider or physical/engineering acceptance is claimed.


### moonproj

R14: The existing MoonFind v1 decision export can be previewed and explicitly prepared as an editable local work draft in the existing multi-draft flow. Exact repeated imports resume retained edits; other drafts remain intact. The portable founder packet retains the full typed research context and current draft. This narrows the local research-intake gap only: before login drafts remain page-local, actor-scoped storage retains existing failure reporting, export-time freshness is unverified, and no formal research-to-task evidence object or signed company decision is created. Live storage/file-picker/accessibility and full authority/privacy/recovery gates remain open.

### moonfind

R14: The unchanged round-two decision-brief/choice-worksheet producer now has a bounded MoonProj consumer that preserves option claims, sources, contradictions, unknowns and falsifying experiments in an editable local draft and founder packet. Actual current/historical/undecided producer exports are checked against the consumer. This does not implement the opposite formal commitment-to-research contract, refresh source truth, perform a customer experiment, authorize a company decision, or promote accepted knowledge.

### moonflow

R14: A pure versioned owner-action projection derives ordered next actions, roles, blockers, exact artifact context, graph revision and event-head identity from retained events. The CLI exposes the same projection shown by Desk. Unrecorded person assignments stay unknown; internal acceptance stays separate from customer outcome. Significant-state/child-history and real CLI parity fixtures add bounded observation evidence, while full persistence-boundary restart/backup qualification and observed human understanding remain open.

### moondesk

R14: The existing Flow work surface now renders a compact owner-action summary with ordered blockers, supporting role when known, exact artifact and revision/event context. The same pure projection is exposed by Flow CLI. It remains a read-only observation and does not assign a person, dispatch work, accept an artifact or prove live freshness. Configured host/browser, full commitment-to-file navigation, accessibility and owning-service authority remain separate gates.

### mooncast

R14: One delivery package can now contain several explicitly named channel cuts with independent exact ranges and rebased SRT intersections. Per-project draft edits/reload/switching and exact synthetic FFmpeg output/package lineage are tested; master and previous delivery bytes remain retained. This supersedes the one-fixed-cut UI gap only. Intended-client artifact access, supported playback/keyboard review, provider/master-input qualification, actual customer acceptance and paid production remain open.

### moonmold

R14: Deterministic cylinders and mixed box/cylinder scenes now export editable semantic source, OBJ, SVG and explicit loss/topology records. Cylinder radius/height, 8-256 sides, translation, mm/cm/m conversion and the supported z-up frame are explicit. Old box-only package member bytes and prior reviews remain unchanged. This supersedes older box-only scope wording only for the bounded mock/reference exporter; arbitrary round-trip import, smooth CAD/booleans, Blender, full resource-envelope/recovery qualification, engineering/physical fitness and actual client acceptance remain open. Broad legacy test failures remain disclosed rather than presented as green.

### Existing requirement additions

#### MOONPROJ-B02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Explicit import preview and Prepare extend the existing local multi-draft flow with known typed MoonFind decision fields. Repeated preparation resumes exact retained edits rather than issuing a formal command; previous drafts and current edits are preserved. The Markdown founder packet retains current draft fields plus original choice, all option assessments, contradictions/unknowns/falsifying experiments and full source register. Stale file reads, cancellation/navigation/actor fences and damaged stored research have reducer-test and file-picker source-review coverage. Full mounted save/reload/Clear/privacy acceptance remains open.

#### MOONPROJ-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Imported research context and its optional commitment reference remain inspectable beside an editable local work draft and in its portable packet. Retained original research is distinct from edited draft text, export-time freshness is explicitly unverified, and no receipt is inferred from a source reference. This is local preparation only: the complete stable-ID source/decision/authorized-work/artifact/customer/cash timeline and its canonical integration receipts remain open.

#### MOONPROJ-N01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: The consumer reconstructs known typed research fields instead of persisting arbitrary imported JSON; unrelated actor/authority/credential metadata is excluded. Source URLs render as escaped text and are not fetched; Prepare has no POST, formal command or authority effect. Free text is not secret-scanned and remains subject to the visible credential warning. This bounded schema/effect fence does not establish the complete browser-storage/export/URL matrix or resolve the older task-URL criterion.

#### MOONFIND-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Actual exports from the unchanged current/historical/undecided worksheet producer round-trip through the Proj typed consumer. All options, strongest-source/claim references, contradictions, unknowns, cost-of-error and falsifying experiments survive local work preparation and portable packet export. No automatic score, scientific validation, current-source refresh or company decision is invented. Mounted end-to-end traceability, evidence quality and actual customer experiments remain open.

#### MOONFLOW-B10

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Owner-action JSON and text CLI output share the versioned pure projection exposed by the existing Desk Flow surface, retaining graph revision, event head, exact artifacts and ordered action/blocker reasons. Seven real CLI histories exercise ready, waiting, review, failed, unknown, accepted and child-revision states and compare bare/status-member output without changing event bytes. These read-only fixtures do not qualify every crash/persistence boundary, corrupt-store recovery or a mounted canvas.

#### MOONFLOW-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: The owner projection covers significant states with ordered next actions, supporting role when derivable and exact artifact/revision/freshness context. Child revisions cannot reuse an earlier completion; no person is invented when event history lacks assignment. Accepted work is explicitly distinguished from customer outcome and the summary is an event-head observation, not proof of live freshness. Source/CLI/render tests do not establish observed reviewer understanding or full live workflow acceptance.

#### MOONDESK-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: The current Flow inspection surface renders the same owner-action projection as the CLI, including unresolved blockers, supporting role, exact artifact context and revision/event head. Unknown assignments and customer outcomes remain unknown, and reading the summary grants no action authority. This improves the bounded work view without proving that a founder can traverse every acceptance-critical file through a configured live commitment workspace.

#### MOONCAST-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Named multi-cut delivery retains independent source variants/time ranges and exact per-output SRT intersections in one versioned package. Synthetic real FFmpeg cuts, ZIP membership and prior master/delivery immutability are checked; per-project draft continuity, invalid ranges and case-insensitive name collisions have source/reducer coverage. Distinct names may intentionally use the same interval. Precise SRT timing does not promise sub-frame media sampling. This is usable local output evidence, not intended-client-role access, authenticated correction acceptance, live review or provider/customer qualification.

#### MOONCAST-N05

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Each selected named cut gets its own range-clipped, zero-rebased caption sidecar, with actual synthetic media/SRT output and package checks. Missing or invalid caption/range cases cannot silently become an acceptable output. Supported playback surfaces and keyboard-accessible mounted review controls have not been exercised, so the authored delivery-accessibility criterion remains partial.

#### MOONMOLD-B01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: The existing typed CreateCylinder operation now reaches deterministic reference delivery with explicit positive integer radius/height, integer sides 8-256, signed integer translation, units and supported frame. Invalid, unsupported or malformed geometry rejects rather than generating fallback output; the UI provides an editable Add cylinder draft. The native operation contract and digital-only ceiling are unchanged. This does not qualify all revision/rejection-history paths, arbitrary operations or live backend execution.

#### MOONMOLD-B02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Cylinder-containing editable source, OBJ and SVG retain exact source lineage, tessellation/topology, nominal dimensions, actual vertex bounds and radial approximation loss. N sides yields 2N vertices, N outward side quads and two outward ngon caps; no smooth normals, booleans or engineering certification are implied. A corrected history-contract edge case now retains the primitive-source contract after replacing a cylinder with a box or resetting the model; genuinely cylinder-free legacy byte compatibility is checked separately. Only the prior box-only exporter gap is superseded; arbitrary representation siblings/import/round-trip fidelity and broad stale-derived-artifact acceptance remain open.

#### MOONMOLD-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Actual mixed and cylinder-primary packages contain editable source, OBJ, SVG, validation/loss/change reports and exact manifest/member digests. Geometry/pack checks, named synthetic review/recovery, no-op lineage and unchanged earlier box/package/review bytes provide bounded digital handoff evidence. Reviewer names are fixtures; actual client review, complete arbitrary-deliverable coverage and full lifecycle remain open.

#### MOONMOLD-N01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R14: Deterministic cylinder/box output converts mm to declared mm/cm/m, keeps centered +Z cylinder axis and z-up-right-handed frame, and derives bounds from actual vertices. Tests cover odd/even sides, translation, mixed index offsets, radius/height and outward winding; nominal diameter is explicitly distinct from odd-sided mesh bounds. The earlier only-boxes statement is superseded for this exporter only. Arbitrary mixed-source import/round-trip fidelity, other frames and physical tolerance qualification remain open.

Evidence cutoff UTC: 2026-10-06T07:35:59Z. Source-assessment and frozen three-file patch identities are recorded separately in the r14 validation receipt. Fish, MoonProp and Mooncake are unchanged/excluded. No baseline, feature candidate, published repository or Mac was changed by this inventory update.

## Bounded round-four feature progress (r15, 2026-10-06)

R15 records four bounded cloud features against the exact delivered round-three baseline: MoonProj local client ZIP preparation from an accepted immutable human-text result; Desk named multi-file Book deliverables; Cast timestamped PNG/JPG covers and retained-image gallery; and Mold typed create/primitive/absolute-move controls. It adds ten requirement notes and seven group next-action notes without changing requirement status, authored acceptance, historical evidence, scope or conditional estimates.

Nine annotated rows remain partial. MOONPROJ-R04 remains planned: a local outbound preparation component does not satisfy authentic payment evidence or unknown-provider reconciliation. The other statuses remain 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified across all 344 requirements, 61 groups and 20 products. Counts are evidence categories, not completion percentages.

The mapping follows the actual authored text. Book B06 receives bounded portable-carrier evidence only; upgrade/rollback remains open. Book UC03 is the original source/knowledge/graph/state projection use-case reflected in B03, not a new inventory requirement. The grouped-file work does not change that projection/timestamp contract, so neither B03 nor UC03 is newly qualified. Desk R01 receives local produced-file organization evidence; the separate B06 HTML Work Package is unchanged. Cast N02's full master-rebuild criterion, mounted accessibility, customer/cash, full lifecycle/learning, provider, installed Mac, live Blender and engineering/physical gates remain open.

Prior r14 and earlier text is retained verbatim as dated history. Only the explicitly named local gaps below are superseded. The original as_of remains 2026-10-04; the later evidence cutoff and immutable feature review are recorded separately. Remaining effort has not been recalculated.


### Bounded final checks

The feature review binds immutable round-four source snapshots. The counts below describe individual verification scopes, may overlap, and are not completion or maturity percentages.

- Proj: independent full OPC UI 416/416, package tests 3/3 on both JS and native, and release JS build pass. Six actual production-generated ZIPs are independently extracted and checked: default two members, optional three members, Unicode filename, and exact empty/BOM/CRLF/multibyte UTF-8 result bytes. SHA-256 is recomputed over decoded bytes. Default client output excludes copied internal task/source/workspace/actor metadata. The local ZIP is preparation only, with no external delivery, customer, invoice or payment receipt
- Desk/Book/Town: independent actual Desk UI workspace 861/861 and release JS build pass, grouped native host tests pass 6/6, and the actual unchanged Book CLI package is imported exactly and checked with no changed members. One Town read-only catalog/GET+HEAD probe covers four output records and all eight files: app HTML/CSS/SVG, report Markdown/SVG, a standalone output and the separate unsigned accepted-human-result source/body. Optional preview/entrypoint fields use the actual producer wire shape, a string when present or omitted, rather than the old documentation's array examples. The worker's existing native export regressions separately pass 14/14. No state/graph/timestamp, HTML Work Package, app execution, upgrade/rollback or live transfer qualification follows
- Cast: independent UI 106/106, release JS build, native execution 5/5 and actual-image HTTP route 1/1 pass. Three covers from a changing synthetic video are PNG/JPG images from master/portrait sources at exact requested times; independently extracted FFmpeg reference bytes match. All 18 ZIP members and checksums are inspected, including retained named cut/exact half-open SRT regressions and unchanged master/SRT/prior archive hashes. Exact-image reads reject wrong project/digest/type and tampering, work before acceptance, and do not create an acceptance side effect. HTTP qualification is in-process native dispatch using explicitly selected Linux FFmpeg, not a deployed or mounted client session
- Mold: independent app UI 47/47 and release JS build pass. Fresh durable native-adapter fixtures use the operations emitted by the compiled typed UI for mm/cm/m. Independent checks cover exact semantic operations, a nine-sided cylinder, OBJ geometry/units/winding/volume/bounds, all eight package member digests, stale-parent rejection and retained earlier downloads. Unsupported advanced JSON remains visible without coercion. Typed controls do not add operations or authority

### Dependency and retained-failure boundaries

Inventory qualification uses the unchanged deterministic importer with its declared async 0.19.4 and x 0.4.50 dependencies. The actual packaged 344-row corpus is parsed in a separate copy of the unmodified dependency-free work_inventory package; four existing tests plus one actual-corpus test run on JS and native. This is a parser-only verification copy, not a substituted feature dependency graph. Native commands are stripped, bounded and serialized; JS checks run outside the native lock. No dependency metadata, source/API, asset or feature implementation is part of the three-file overlay.

The feature review checks each resolved registry version against physical dependency metadata and retains the actual full Desk workspace. Proj resolves x 0.4.50/async 0.19.4/MoonLib 0.1.19/Lepusa 0.1.4/Rabbita 0.12.4. Mold uses x 0.4.45 at root and Rabbita 0.12.4 for UI. Cast root uses x 0.4.50/async 0.19.4/MoonLib 0.1.19; UI uses x 0.4.40/Rabbita 0.12.4. Desk uses x 0.4.50/async 0.19.4/Lepusa 0.1.6/MoonLib 0.1.22/MoonLeaf 0.1.15/moonzip 0.2.4/fzip 0.8.5/Rabbita 0.12.4. Town uses x 0.4.50/async 0.19.4/Lepusa 0.1.4/MoonLib 0.1.19. The reused Book CLI's exact 552-file source/tree and binary digest were independently verified; it was not rebuilt during the review. Town production source is unchanged; its two reviewer-only probe files are not product changes.

Mold's Vite asset bundling remains unverified because vite was absent; MoonBit release JS compilation passed. No optimized native Studio release build is claimed for Cast. Cover timestamps retain millisecond input but extraction follows the encoded source frame cadence, and dimensions follow the chosen source rather than legacy nominal width/height fields.

All older qualification records remain history. The historical broad Mold Node failures for missing Blender, Mac-only paths and external-image assumptions remain unresolved by typed-control work. Earlier dependency-wide Rabbita DOM/URL failures without a browser and Cast standalone native release optimization stopped without an executable are not retroactively passing. Existing compiler/dependency warnings remain. No Vite distribution, mounted browser, installed Mac, configured provider, actual client transmission/access/acceptance, payment, publication or engineering/physical acceptance is claimed.


### moonproj

R15: An internally accepted immutable human-revision UTF-8 result can now be previewed and prepared as a local client ZIP containing original result bytes and a friendly README. The filename and handoff note are editable; technical source JSON is optional and excluded by default. Only retained text from 0 through 256 KiB is supported; extensions do not convert bytes, binary/original/continuation outputs remain unsupported, and notes are page-local. This narrows outbound artifact preparation only. It does not transmit the packet, create delivery/customer/invoice/payment evidence, change company state or satisfy external acceptance and reconciled cash. Full live authority, browser/privacy/recovery and authentic customer/cash gates remain open.

### moonbook

R15: The unchanged Book portable-pack producer can carry explicit grouped Desk deliverables, preserving report/app relative files plus optional preview and entrypoint references; actual local pack/import and Town inert catalog/download qualification is bounded to that carrier. This adds portable-output usability, not pack upgrade/merge/rollback qualification or source/graph/state/timestamp changes. Book factual review, authorized knowledge scope, cross-machine deployment, runtime activation and measured learning remain separate.

### moondesk

R15: The existing Book handoff can group produced files under explicit deliverable names while retaining relative asset paths and optional preview/app entrypoint references. Grouped output uses existing local pack/inspect and digest-bound downloads; flat exports and accepted-human-result support remain. This narrows local output organization and discoverability only. The separate HTML Work Package, full commitment/receipt navigation, configured host/browser, cross-machine transfer, runtime activation, accessibility and owning-service authority remain open.

### mooncast

R15: Studio delivery drafts now select up to eight named timestamped PNG/JPG covers from the master or horizontal/vertical variant. Completed builds expose exact digest-bound image previews/downloads and include the same bytes in their ZIP; retained earlier builds remain unchanged. Per-project drafts, stale-master acknowledgement, invalid inputs and interrupted/repeated actions have bounded compiled coverage. This supersedes the missing timestamp/format/gallery UI slice only. Actual recipient access, supported playback/keyboard review, provider/master-input qualification, paid production, client acceptance and publication remain open.

### moonmold

R15: Labeled create-model, box, cylinder and absolute-move fields now prepare the existing semantic operations with explicit integer-millimetre dimensions/positions and mm/cm/m representation units. Invalid/incomplete fields block dispatch; busy/stale-parent gates and old downloads/reviews are preserved. Advanced JSON remains available without coercing unsupported schemas; copy-last-operation prepares only a new draft. This narrows input usability, not modeling authority or operation scope. The geometry remains bounded mock/reference output; arbitrary CAD/import, mounted browser, full recovery/resource envelope, live Blender, engineering/physical fitness and actual client acceptance remain open. Broad legacy environment failures remain disclosed.

### Existing requirement additions

#### MOONPROJ-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: The exact internally accepted human-revision result can be inspected and carried as unchanged UTF-8 bytes in a local client ZIP with editable basename/note and an optional original technical source. Accepted report/task/revision context fences stale reads and previews; preparing or downloading performs no company mutation. This provides an artifact-preparation link only. It does not complete the stable-ID source/decision/work/customer/cash timeline or establish recipient delivery, owner/receipt completeness or live integration recovery.

#### MOONPROJ-R04

Status: planned -> planned (unchanged)

R15: A local ZIP now prepares an internally accepted human-text result for possible outbound delivery; default content is the original result and a friendly README, with provenance JSON opt-in. Actual extracted-byte fixtures cover Unicode/CRLF/BOM, empty content and exact digest rejection. No transmission or external delivery/customer receipt is recorded, and no invoice/payment/provider reconciliation is implemented. The authored paid-state and unknown-provider-outcome criteria remain unfulfilled, so this broad external-acceptance/reconciled-cash row stays planned despite the supporting local packaging component.

#### MOONBOOK-B06

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Unchanged local Book pack machinery can carry grouped generated report/app outputs selected through Desk, retaining each relative member and optional preview/entrypoint metadata. Actual Book producer, exact local import and Town catalog/inert-download fixtures provide bounded portable-carrier evidence. The full authored reviewed upgrade/rollback, conflicts, installed accepted-pack preservation and cross-machine deployment criteria are not newly qualified.

#### MOONDESK-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Explicit named groups organize selected output members and preserve report/app relative asset paths with optional preview/app entrypoint references through the existing Book package route. This improves produced-file discovery within that local handoff; ordinary flat exports and accepted-human-result packaging remain. It does not complete MoonProj commitment binding, every acceptance-critical link/receipt, configured host/browser navigation, mounted file opening or unavailable-record recovery. The separate HTML Work Package and Book state/graph/timestamp projections are unchanged.

#### MOONCAST-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Named timestamped PNG/JPG covers from exact master/variant sources are exposed as build-bound digest/length/type-checked local previews and individual downloads, and the identical image bytes remain in the delivery ZIP beside the existing master/captions/labels. Prior builds retain their own files. Synthetic changing-video frame extraction and ZIP comparisons support this package component; intended-client access, complete required-artifact failure behavior and corrected client-accepted version workflows remain separate.

#### MOONCAST-N05

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: The local cover gallery adds readable name/source/requested-time labels and exact retained-image download links, with compiled rendered-HTML escaping and historical-build tests. The existing caption/rights/AI-label outputs remain. This is source/rendered and synthetic-media coverage only; supported playback surfaces and keyboard-accessible review controls have not been exercised as a mounted user journey.

#### MOONMOLD-B01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Typed fields prepare only the existing create-model, box, cylinder and absolute-move semantic requests. Positive dimensions, signed movement and side-count/native integer bounds are explicit; incomplete/invalid typed input blocks Save/Validate/Execute. Recognized exact JSON loads into typed fields; unknown methods/extra fields/unsupported frames remain advanced JSON without coercion and host validation stays authoritative. Compiled-operation/native-adapter parity narrows input preparation, without adding an arbitrary script/shell API or newly qualifying every rejected-revision lifecycle.

#### MOONMOLD-B02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Whole-millimetre typed dimensions and absolute positions feed the existing mm/cm/m editable-source, OBJ and SVG outputs. Changing fields invalidates current validation and keeps prior exported artifacts/reviews historical and byte-retained. Actual durable mock-adapter fixtures compare semantic inputs, representation geometry and member digests; unsupported siblings, full conversion-loss closure and broader stale-sibling/recovery acceptance remain open.

#### MOONMOLD-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Labeled object dimensions, model/export units and signed absolute move controls reduce ambiguity when preparing operations after the existing digital brief. Copy-last-operation is an editable new draft with current parent/request identity, not an implicit execution or reuse of approval. This is bounded input usability only: it does not establish a complete stable founder commitment, real client constraints/tolerances/intended-use review or renewed customer acceptance.

#### MOONMOLD-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R15: Operations emitted from the compiled typed-control fixture are exercised through the durable native mock adapter and checked against editable-source/OBJ/SVG, exact file hashes and retained prior downloads across supported representation units. Existing digital delivery and named review retain their authority boundaries. This extends the validated local package input path; actual client opening, required-source/loss-report delivery blocking and corrected named client acceptance remain separate.

Evidence cutoff UTC: 2026-10-06T12:55:37Z. Source-assessment and frozen three-file patch identities are recorded separately in the r15 validation receipt. Fish, MoonProp and Mooncake are unchanged/excluded. No baseline, feature candidate, published repository or Mac was changed by this inventory update.

## Bounded round-five feature progress (r16, 2026-10-06)

R16 records four bounded cloud features against the exact delivered round-four baseline: MoonProj's read-only commitment journey; MoonBook's pre-task reviewed-lesson browser; MoonFind's evidence comparison and continued worksheet with paired Proj history preservation; and MoonMold's structured digital brief, revision and retained-package recovery. It adds eight requirement notes and five group next-action notes without changing requirement status, authored acceptance, historical evidence, scope or conditional estimates.

All eight annotated rows remain partial. Totals remain 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified across 344 requirements, 61 groups and 20 products. Counts are evidence categories, not completion percentages.

Mapping follows the actual authored text. Proj R01 receives read-only receipt navigation; R02 receives bounded historical research-packet preservation, without claiming an offer or company mutation. Book R02/R03 receive planning discovery and reviewed-history usability. Find R02 receives evidence-to-choice continuity; its B04 host manifest/ZIP and B03 configured child-run contracts are unchanged. Mold B08/R01/R02 receive exact draft/revision/package-history evidence. No use-case ID is invented as an inventory requirement. Book's source-ingest trust and B03/UC03 projection/timestamp contract are unchanged. Proj R04 remains planned; external acceptance and cash are still explicit gaps.

Prior r15 and earlier text is retained verbatim as dated history. Only explicitly named local gaps below are superseded. The original as_of remains 2026-10-04; later evidence cutoff and immutable feature review are recorded separately. Remaining effort has not been recalculated. No mounted-browser, live provider, installed Mac, actual customer, measured learning, Blender or physical qualification is inferred.


### Bounded final checks

The immutable feature review binds seven exact product snapshots, including the combined Proj journey/Find consumer and changed Book. Individual counts below can overlap and are not completion or maturity percentages.

- Proj: independent combined OPC UI 428/428, task-evidence 40/40 on JS and native, research parser 9/9 on JS and native, release JS build and target-scoped info pass. The combined source contains disjoint journey and research-consumer changes. Actual production-generated receipt data reaches decode/apply/render; causal stages retain missing/out-of-order times, exact proposal/report/revision identity and visible uncertain last-known reads. No company command, external delivery, acceptance, invoice or cash fact is added
- Book: independent native store 73/73, UI 119/119 with the real backend-wire test body enabled, and release JS build pass. A fresh native CLI process reproduces reviewed cards from the durable journal; empty-root browsing does not initialize a store. The worker separately passes stripped non-release native host and lesson CLI builds plus the exact native-wire UI test. Search and original/history navigation preserve pending draft/task context and leave trust/review/rollback/attachment policy unchanged
- Find/Proj: independent Find UI 106/106 and release JS build pass. Production Find exports for current, changed, removed and retrieval-only-refreshed evidence feed the paired Proj parser and local draft store/restore/packet tests. Material changes clear active references to Unknown while exact old receipts and complete outgoing user worksheets remain historical; retrieval-only refresh retains current selections. The existing v1 envelope/statuses remain compatible. Twenty-history/two-million-character backup bounds stop continuation rather than evicting evidence. This is local same-run worksheet continuity, not child research execution, a host delivery manifest or a company decision
- Mold: the independent UI runner reports 59/59, consisting of 58 exercised ordinary tests and one optional bridge case explicitly marked not-run-without-host-fixture. That bridge is separately exercised three times with actual native response bytes. Release JS, three durable native brief/revision/package/recovery journeys for mm/cm/m, and all three native-to-UI brief bridges pass. First reviewed bundle/brief/download bytes stay exact after revised geometry; new output does not inherit review. Worker regression scopes separately pass 46 native-backed delivery/typed-object cases and 12 core tests on each of four targets

### Dependency and retained-failure boundaries

Inventory qualification uses the byte-unchanged deterministic importer with its declared async 0.19.4 and x 0.4.50 dependencies. The actual packaged 344-row corpus is parsed in a separate copy of the unmodified dependency-free work_inventory package: four existing tests plus one actual-corpus test on JS and native. This parser-only copy does not replace any feature dependency graph. Native commands are stripped, bounded to one job, serialized by the shared lock and given a 64 MiB stack; JS checks do not take the native lock. No dependency declaration, source/API or product asset is part of the three-file overlay.

Feature review records exact resolved graphs and physical cached dependencies: Proj uses x 0.4.50, async 0.19.4, MoonLib 0.1.19, Lepusa 0.1.4 and Rabbita 0.12.4; Book root uses x 0.4.50, async 0.19.4 and MoonLib 0.1.19, and its UI uses Rabbita 0.12.4; Find UI uses Rabbita 0.12.4; Mold ordinary root uses x 0.4.45 and its UI uses Rabbita 0.12.4. Desk, Flow and Cast source trees are unchanged from the delivered round-four baseline; their prior qualifications remain history.

Book's optional optimized native host build was deliberately interrupted with exit 130; only the stripped non-release host build is qualified. The giant native wiki suite was not run. Mold's full Vite distribution attempt still fails with vite not found (exit 127), although MoonBit release JS compiles. Historical broad Mold Node/Blender/Mac-path failures, earlier dependency-wide browser DOM/URL failures and stopped optimized Cast Studio work are not retroactively passing. Existing compiler warnings remain.

No mounted browser, actual storage/download/accessibility session, installed Mac, signed live service/PostgreSQL, configured provider, real client transmission/opening/acceptance, payment, measured learning, publication, live Blender or engineering/physical qualification is claimed. All native review identities and customer-like inputs in these checks are synthetic. Unpackaged frozen-brief recovery and complete lifecycle/resource envelopes remain open.


### moonproj

R16: A read-only seven-stage commitment journey brings existing source, proposal/recorded feedback, formal work, saved result/report, internal acceptance, external acceptance gap and cash gap together without changing company state. Stages are causal work order, not invented chronology; missing dates and uncertain reads remain explicit, and saved proposal/report/revision identities stay distinct. Paired research consumption now preserves Find's optional continued-worksheet history and exact unresolved historical source/claim receipts in local packets without treating them as current evidence or a company decision. Full receipt/owner coverage, live signed service, mounted browser, external acceptance and reconciled cash remain open.

### moonbook

R16: The existing Bookkeeper screen can search and inspect current reviewed lessons before a future task exists, showing applicability, invalidations, named review and exact source references. Other saved/proposed/rejected/historical records remain separately inspectable, and original/history navigation preserves draft and task context. Exact later-task retrieval and explicit attachment remain the existing authority path; text similarity does not establish applicability. The browser is read-only and does not initialize an empty store. Source-ingest trust, acceptance, rollback and task-scope policy are unchanged. Mounted browser, full lifecycle, real customer learning and measured improvement remain open.

### moonfind

R16: A deliberate local continuation previews added/changed/removed or retrieval-refreshed evidence, archives the outgoing worksheet and carries user criteria, assessments, choice and reasoning as unverified user content. Materially changed, removed or unresolved active citations become Unknown while exact old receipts remain historical; retrieval-time-only refresh preserves the existing active selection with its earlier receipt archived. Missing projection is unavailable, not removal. Full JSON backup and per-version JSON/Markdown keep bounded history, and the paired Proj parser/packet retains the additive v1 fields. This is same-run worksheet continuity, not a provider rerun, host delivery manifest, company decision or source-truth acceptance.

### moonmold

R16: Labeled fields now prepare the existing digital brief: commitment reference, revision, intended use, integer-mm dimensions, representation units, constraints and criteria. Start-next-revision copies the draft; explicit selected-package load restores verified retained brief bytes. Unsupported JSON remains advanced text without coercion, incomplete fields block freezing and separate dirty/generation guards protect pending drafts from operation/load races. Durable mock fixtures retain older package/review bytes through revised briefs and recovery; new output does not inherit review. Unpackaged-frozen-brief recovery, arbitrary geometry, live browser/Blender, actual client acceptance and engineering/physical fitness remain open. Earlier broad environment limits are retained.

### Existing requirement additions

#### MOONPROJ-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: The existing task now has a read-only seven-stage commitment journey linking source, exact proposal/recorded feedback, formal work, result/report and internal acceptance, while explicitly leaving external acceptance and invoice/cash unconnected. It distinguishes current source from creation context, proposal-version feedback from unrelated responses, recorder from claimed respondent/time, and accepted report/source from the inspected report. Causal ordering preserves absent/out-of-order timestamps; stale/failed reads keep visibly uncertain last-known records, and actor/task changes clear old evidence. This narrows receipt navigation only, not complete stable-ID owner/receipt coverage, authenticated external claims or live integration recovery.

#### MOONPROJ-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

This supports retaining learning while a local research basis changes; it creates no company decision, buyer observation, offer revision, demand proof, external acceptance or financial state. The complete factual-buyer and deployed founder acceptance remains open.

#### MOONBOOK-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: A pre-task reviewed-lesson browser exposes current named-human-reviewed lessons, exact source/review references, full applicability and invalidation conditions before a new task identity exists. Search is bounded read-only discovery; real task-scoped retrieval and explicit attachment remain separate and similarity is not proof of applicability. This narrows planning retrieval without modifying outcome evidence, changing trust defaults, automatically closing conflicting receipts or establishing real customer learning.

#### MOONBOOK-R03
