R16: Current lesson cards retain the named reviewer and exact reviewed lineage, while matching proposed/rejected/historical and ambiguous legacy versions remain separately inspectable without becoming current guidance. Original/history navigation preserves in-progress draft and received task context; independent record-read failures require recovery before navigation. Existing reviewed revision/rollback and task applicability remain covered by durable regressions but their authority is unchanged. This is discovery and history usability, not a new approval path, source-trust migration, cross-machine recovery or measured learning qualification.

#### MOONFIND-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: Compare-updated-evidence shows exact added/changed/removed and retrieval-refreshed sources/claims/analysis before an explicit same-run continuation. The outgoing worksheet is archived; criteria, assessments, direction, reasoning and next action carry only as user-authored content. Materially changed/removed/unresolved citations clear to Unknown with exact earlier receipts retained as historical; retrieval-only refresh preserves the active citation without claiming renewed truth. Unavailable/ambiguous evidence blocks misleading continuation, history/backup limits stop before silent loss, and per-version JSON/Markdown plus paired Proj consumption retain prior context. Full scientific review, configured research execution and actual founder/customer decisions remain open.

#### MOONMOLD-B08

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: Explicit loading of a selected retained package's verified brief prepares an editable draft without altering the original frozen brief, immutable exports or named review. Starting a revision copies current content without executing or inheriting approval; dirty/generation/package guards reject stale loads and protect pending brief edits across operation recovery. Actual local native-to-UI fixtures retain first reviewed bundles and require separate review for revised output. Synthetic reviewer history is not real client acceptance or mounted-browser review usability.

#### MOONMOLD-R01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: Structured controls expose the existing commitment reference, revision, intended use, integer-mm dimensions, units, constraints and review criteria without inventing schema fields. Fixed deliverables/frame/digital-only authority remain visible. Exact round-trip hydration preserves supported content; unknown schemas, unusual arrays or out-of-range values remain advanced JSON without coercion, and incomplete typed input cannot freeze stale complete JSON. Revision-from-draft and selected-package restoration preserve content. This narrows digital brief preparation/revision, not authenticated commitment binding, ambiguous tolerance resolution or actual designer/client acceptance.

#### MOONMOLD-R02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R16: Compiled brief controls feed three durable mock native journeys in mm/cm/m through first package, named fixture review, revised brief/geometry, second package and recovery. Exact old brief/bundle/review downloads are retained and current output never inherits old acceptance; real native file-route bytes rehydrate the production UI draft. This adds bounded revision/recovery evidence for the existing local digital package. Arbitrary requested deliverables, complete required-source/loss blocking, real client opening/corrected acceptance, live backend and engineering fitness remain separate.

Evidence cutoff UTC: 2026-10-06T15:08:00Z. Source-assessment and frozen three-file patch identities are recorded separately in the r16 validation receipt. Fish, MoonProp and Mooncake are unchanged/excluded. No baseline, feature candidate, published repository or Mac was changed by this inventory update.

## Bounded round-six feature progress (r17, 2026-10-06)

This revision records three newly changed runtime products: MoonGate saved-provider model-catalog selection, MoonClaw run-owned retained-file downloads with necessary session-only gateway authentication, and MoonFort exact retained command-output API/CLI retrieval with one-pass raw export. MoonProj has no new feature code in this round. Its only source changes are this inventory, the generated assessment file and the inventory guide.

The exact round-six baseline contains the seven cumulative delivered round-five product sources and nine unchanged less-touched repository snapshots. New Gate/Claw/Fort candidates and their reviewer evidence are pinned separately below. This does not imply the historical published heads were freshly rechecked or the Mac is in parity.

Eight existing requirements and seven existing work groups receive additive notes. Seven selected requirements remain partial, and MOONCLAW-B06 stays unverified: session authentication is a prerequisite, not version negotiation or remote-identity reconnect acceptance. All 344 requirements, 61 groups and 20 products keep their authored criteria, history, identities, scopes, membership and original conditional estimate arithmetic. Status totals stay 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and 1 verified. No group status changes. Remaining effort has not been recalculated.

Claw serves current retained-file bytes for an exact record/run without a digest-backed immutable-version guarantee. Fort signed output references remain separate from Claw ArtifactStore/UI downloads. Catalog discovery does not run inference, select an active route or prove live readiness. Source, local HTTP/filesystem/CLI, UI reducer and DOM-save fixtures remain distinct from mounted browser, actual provider, production host, Mac, deployment/containment, external customer/cash and full lifecycle/privacy acceptance.


### Independently pinned feature evidence and limits

Each final feature receipt names its independently reconstructed source tree, complete source and metadata manifests, exact dependency graph, physical dependency hashes and immutable evidence manifest. The r17 pinning step verifies all three full evidence manifests and the owner candidates against the exact reviewer source manifests before generation. Inventory validation is a separate narrow check, not a rerun or enlargement of feature qualification.

MoonGate: independent fresh stripped-native catalog tests passed 4/4 and production JavaScript VM behavioral tests passed 29/29. The real local registered management route/security middleware was exercised against a fake provider with fixture-only credentials: unauthorized calls made no upstream request; saved credentials and endpoint were used; override attempts, malformed/absent catalogs and credential-bearing data were rejected or redacted. Production decoder/store reload and JavaScript draft-selection/save/reload legs are separately qualified, not a mounted end-to-end browser test. Provider pagination beyond the returned bounded catalog is not claimed. The owner full native run is 910/912: two unchanged OpenCode/Hermes SQLite-session tests fail with sqlite3 absent, independently checked against baseline source and PATH. The strict gate has 104 legacy warning-as-error diagnostics; its exact log is retained. Normal checks/focused tests are not a warning-clean/full-suite pass.

MoonFort: independent fresh bounded stripped-native reader build passed and nine focused public-protocol/output-reader/artifact tests passed 9/9. The actual CLI emitted exactly 2 MiB to raw stdout and a separately read-back file; JSON pages, Unicode/NUL, repeated/final/empty pages and budgets passed. Wrong bindings, forged/missing/expired/substituted objects and partial-cursor/unsupported-argument/missing-config/malformed/incomplete/oversized-request refusals are explicit. Pre-write raw-export validation refusals emit no artifact bytes, return nonzero and report on stderr. Output-device failures after a write begins are not covered by that empty-stdout guarantee. The full exporter verifies the object once and writes raw bytes once; JSON paging deliberately reverifies each page. The reviewer observed a 292 ms 2 MiB fixture sample, not an SLA. Grants and scratch stayed untouched. Compiler warnings remain; no sandbox workload, containment, strict deployment, Claw UI mapping or installed-host qualification is established.

MoonClaw: final independently accepted tree 1731c6044da9bfcb0653678db1d3d8810ad69cd3 passed four bounded stripped-native tests and 59 compiled UI/reducer/transport tests, plus JS release and Vite builds. The actual Gateway-to-shipped-download transport uses real loopback HTTP and a fixture token, with only DOM save stubbed. The actual ordinary Unicode text GET-to-shipped-decoder-to-ArtifactLoaded-to-editor path preserves exact text after a narrow Option-array compatibility correction; the superseded tree's old array-rendering mismatch does not describe the final tree. The integration test is skipped in the standalone UI count and explicitly run in the native fixture, so those overlapping counts must not be summed. Binary/NUL/invalid-UTF-8/empty/Unicode bytes, exact ownership, missing-file recovery, repeat/cancel/stale behavior and the sparse 64 MiB limit are bounded fixtures. The changed-length guard is source-reviewed, without arbitrary concurrent-write or same-length mutation detection. Session-only token scope and malformed-token canaries are tested. No content digest or immutable-storage guarantee is introduced. The independent JS/Vite rebuild differs in six individually checked Rabbita diagnostic source-position strings; normalized comparison passes, while byte-identical bundle reproducibility is not claimed. Exact owner assets and the baseline build label were restored and rehashed. No full-native or warning-clean claim, mounted browser/OS save, cross-origin CORS, authenticated EventSource deployment, real credentials/provider, Mac or Fort spool mapping is established.

The original importer is unchanged. R17 checks exact r16 reproduction, new r17 generation and byte-for-byte --check against every authored PRD definition and packaged body; the unchanged parser's four tests plus a real 344-row portfolio test run on both native and JS. Interface/format checks, all packaged product assets, unchanged source hashes, complete prior annotation history, exact statuses and all estimate arithmetic are verified separately. No full Proj build, provider, Mac or live browser is implied by the inventory check.


### moongate

R17: A saved-provider model catalog can be refreshed and one returned model ID explicitly selected for supported OpenAI-compatible/Anthropic API-key providers; manual model entry remains available. The host resolves the saved provider and holds its key, bounds catalog responses and returns validated model IDs with redacted failure messages. Discovery does not perform inference, consume inference quota, select an active route, change app bindings or prove readiness. Missing configuration, unsupported providers and stale UI requests remain explicit. Next qualify a named actual client/provider, real browser first-run and the full routing, secret, health, failover and policy gates. Strict warning qualification remains blocked by baseline warnings; ordinary checks and focused tests do not erase that limitation.

### moonclaw

R17: Run detail adds Download for exact run-owned retained artifacts, including binary bytes, with an authenticated read-only gateway route and bounded browser transport. Same-origin gateway UI supports a manually supplied token held only in tab memory and sent to the selected exact gateway /v1/ path; token/base changes cancel or invalidate pending work. Download does not use unsaved editor text, rerun work, accept output or promote knowledge. Exact record/run identity is enforced, but retained bytes are current file contents: the artifact schema has no digest contract or immutable-history guarantee. Missing/unreadable/oversize files, cancellation and retry remain explicit. Synthetic workspace previews still require an actual retained artifact. Cross-origin CORS, authenticated event streaming, mounted browser/OS-save, protocol-version/reconnect and complete export/privacy/recovery qualification remain open. Fort signed output references are not mapped to Claw artifacts.

### moonfort

R17: A trusted read-command-output API/CLI retrieves retained output through the same receipt's exact signed reference, run ID, approval digest and command digest. Typed byte pages support inspection and a single-pass raw export verifies the full retained object once before writing bytes. Changed/substituted, expired, missing or mismatched references fail; pre-write validation refusals emit no artifact bytes, return nonzero and report on stderr. Existing limits and expiry apply, and no arbitrary host path, grant consumption, command replay, promotion or retention extension is added. This closes a bounded retained-output inspection gap only. It is a CLI/API feature without Claw/Desk output mapping, deployment qualification, sandbox workload execution or containment evidence; full storage exhaustion, retention operations, privacy, host recovery and strict AEN/microVM gates remain open. Shell redirection may create an empty file before a refused read, so exit status remains required.

### Existing requirement additions

#### MOONGATE-B01

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: Saved-provider catalog discovery offers returned model IDs and explicit selection while retaining manual entry and the distinction between edited and saved configuration. Supported API-key providers are resolved host-side; unsupported or missing configuration blocks discovery with a specific message. Catalog lookup sends no inference request and does not select an active route or establish eligible dispatch. The actual compatible-client, policy, missing-model/credential and safe retry acceptance remains broader and open.

#### MOONGATE-B02

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: The catalog route uses the saved provider's host-held key, returns only validated/deduplicated IDs, removes arbitrary upstream metadata and refuses secret-containing IDs. Bounded malformed/error responses use fixed messages instead of forwarding upstream bodies. Native loopback canary fixtures qualify this new catalog boundary only; complete client traffic, telemetry, host storage and actual dispatch removal/restoration acceptance remain open.

#### MOONGATE-N03

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: The model picker labels catalog refresh as discovery, shows recoverable empty/error/unsupported states, retains manual entry and fences older requests when provider context changes. Discovery does not claim syntax validation, successful inference or live readiness. UI behavioral fixtures and generated/source wiring do not substitute for the authored observed clean first-run qualification with an ordinary operator and real eligible routes.

#### MOONCLAW-B06

Status: unverified -> unverified (unchanged)

R17: A prerequisite for protected download and snapshot reads is added: explicit gateway token entry in the same-origin UI, memory-only retention, Authorization scoped to the exact selected gateway and /v1/ paths, and token/base-change invalidation of pending reads. It uses the existing gateway Bearer check and adds no persistent credential access. This does not implement or qualify supported ACP/gateway version negotiation, unsupported-version refusal, original remote-identity reconnect, cross-origin deployment or authenticated event streaming. The requirement therefore remains unverified.

#### MOONCLAW-B10

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: An explicit run-detail Download retrieves the retained file for exact artifact ID and owning run, preserving empty/Unicode/NUL/non-UTF-8 bytes through the bounded attachment route. Gateway auth precedes lookup; absent/wrong/invalid identity and missing/unreadable/oversize files fail visibly. Duplicate pending clicks, cancellation, timeout, stale results and retry are handled without rerunning work. The button reports browser handoff rather than proof of OS save and never exports unsaved editor text. The current artifact schema has no content digest: out-of-band edits or mutable updates may change retained bytes, so this is not immutable historical export or tamper detection. Complete plan/authority/journal export, secrets policy, identity-preserving reimport and live browser acceptance remain separate.

#### MOONFORT-B06

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: The receipt-bound reader exposes retained command output beyond display truncation, with bounded JSON byte pages and complete one-pass raw export. It preserves existing retention expiry and hard output ceiling, distinguishes unavailable/refused reads, and neither replays the command nor consumes authority. Bounded real CLI/file fixtures qualify output inspection only; timeout enforcement, aggregate resource controls, backend-specific storage exhaustion and shared-kernel limits are not newly qualified.

#### MOONFORT-B09

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: A signed command-output reference from the exact completed receipt can be read through the typed API/CLI, reconstructing retained full bytes including Unicode and NUL; full raw export verifies once and writes once rather than looping through pages. Empty output, byte cursors/EOF, missing files, expiry, reference mismatch and substituted bytes have explicit success/refusal fixtures. Pre-write raw-export validation refusals emit no artifact bytes, return nonzero and report on stderr, although shell redirection may already create an empty destination. No new scratch inventory, spool-writing/retention extension or automatic Claw artifact mapping is introduced; the full production spool-failure/retention acceptance remains open.

#### MOONFORT-N03

Status: partial -> partial (unchanged)

R17: Retained-output reads bind the signed reference to the same run, approval digest and command digest and recheck retained bytes; wrong bindings, forged references and byte substitution refuse. Retrieval leaves grants, execution and scratch promotion untouched and cannot upgrade the receipt's backend enforcement level. This supplies a bounded artifact-reference integrity slice; complete task/grant/backend/scope receipt tamper coverage, deployment containment and external host reconciliation remain separate.

### Existing group additions

- moongate-route (status/estimate unchanged): R17 completed locally: saved-provider catalog refresh-and-selection and its host-secret/error boundary. Next qualify the actual named client/provider and full routing/stream/failover/canary matrix; catalog discovery is not inference or dispatch readiness.
- moongate-spending (status/estimate unchanged): R17 adds only model-picker usability evidence associated with N03. Observe the full clean first-run operator workflow; spending reservations, policy activation/rollback, price truth and commitment receipts are unchanged.
- moonclaw-authority (status/estimate unchanged): R17 adds session-only exact-gateway authentication transport needed for protected reads. Next qualify supported version negotiation, identity-preserving reconnect and full secrets/authority boundaries; memory-only token fixtures do not fulfill those wider criteria.
- moonclaw-durable (status/estimate unchanged): R17 completed locally: read-only run-owned retained-file downloads with explicit failures and stale/cancel/retry fencing. Next qualify mounted save behavior and complete evidence export/reimport; retain the lack of a content-digest immutable-history contract and leave Fort spool mapping separate.
- moonfort-local (status/estimate unchanged): R17 completed locally: retained-output inspection beyond display truncation, including a bounded full raw export. Next measure production output/spool/retention capacity and exhaustion on named hosts; retrieval fixtures do not prove containment, timeout or aggregate quota enforcement.
- moonfort-promotion (status/estimate unchanged): R17 completed locally: exact signed retained command-output API/CLI retrieval without execution or promotion. Next qualify host presentation and operational retention/privacy; no Claw/Desk button or automatic spool-to-artifact mapping is part of this slice, and promotion authority remains unchanged.
- moonfort-grants (status/estimate unchanged): R17 adds artifact-reference integrity checks binding output to exact run/approval/command digests and detects substituted bytes. Keep separate the wider grant, process-start, scope and enforcement qualification; reading retained bytes consumes no grant and starts no workload.

## Bounded round-seven authoring progress (r18, 2026-10-06)

Four existing requirements and two existing groups receive additive evidence. All 20 products, 344 requirements, 61 groups, authored criteria, statuses, history, scope and conditional estimates are preserved. Totals remain 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and one verified; no maturity percentage or revised remaining-effort estimate is inferred. The exact round-six Proj tree is the base. Only the generated inventory and these two existing guides change.

R18 evidence is bounded cloud source, compiled contract/reducer/render/transport fixtures, native filesystem authoring and portable pending-bundle qualification. It does not establish an executed collaboration, semantic synthesis or contradiction discovery, actual human decision, Book-owner acceptance, provider execution, receiver delivery, mounted-browser/keyboard/accessibility, installed Mac, publication or business outcome. Test counts overlap and are not product completion measurements.

### moontown

R18: Request Desk now authors a one-off collaboration plan from a question, at least two existing Books, explicit source paths, participant assignments and named human decision owners. Incomplete drafts can be saved and reopened; prepare produces pending civic/Chat handoffs and participant briefs, with captured source bytes and deterministic portable export. Exact revision retry, interrupted writes, source/generated-artifact tamper, stale callbacks and reopened-revision reuse are qualified in controlled fixtures. Reuse creates a new draft and recaptures current files without carrying decisions. This prepares coordination work only: no synthesis is invented, no Book is provisioned or mutated, and no provider, worker, scheduler, collaboration execution or real human acceptance occurs. The complete civic/spatial intervention, sourced synthesis, cross-book contradiction/owner return and observed operational outcomes remain open.

### moonchat

R18: Pure review authoring helpers construct question, objection and proposal entries with explicit human decision owners, bind pending bundles to observed source bytes, serialize through the existing wire decoder, and supersede using exact retained predecessor bytes plus both observed source versions. Old decisions/history are preserved without inheriting receipts into a new pending bundle. Actual Town producer output is accepted by the production native adapter in pending and separate test-only approve/reject/defer variants. Town owns constituent snapshot verification; Chat independently binds only its main source and predecessor bundle. The hosted Proj/Find workflow named in R01 is not implemented or newly qualified by this Town extension. No standalone Chat app or actual reviewer decision is added. Core/bundle/operation identities stay unchanged; the static base-schema value is aligned with the existing MoonLib moonsuite-conversation.v2 runtime, and x0.4.50 addresses current toolchain compatibility. Full receiver, keyboard/accessibility and ordinary-user host acceptance remains open.

### MOONTOWN-B03

Status remains unverified. R18: The authoring UI and host prepare a one-off civic handoff, participant briefs and pending Chat review bundle from explicit existing Books, captured source bytes and named human decision owners. Missing/invalid sources refuse and the portable evidence remains source-bound. This is preparation for a sourced synthesis; it does not run the civic synthesis operation, generate a synthesis, deliver to a curator or establish owning-Book acceptance. The complete authored synthesis outcome remains unverified.

### MOONTOWN-B05

Status remains unverified. R18: The collaboration composer resolves saved drafts and prepared exports to content-derived durable revisions; optimistic revision checks, dirty/superseded callbacks, navigation fencing and exact reopened-revision reuse prevent tested stale actions from silently targeting newer work. Reopen revalidates saved draft identity and captured sources; prepare retry, export and download revalidate generated briefs, bundle and handoff. The download bridge checks exact bytes. This adds a bounded draft-authoring/freshness slice, not the full civic/spatial supervisor-intervention or every actionable-item acceptance. Mounted frontend/backend intervention and owning-service authority remain unverified.

### MOONTOWN-R02

Status remains planned. R18: A user may explicitly author an objection with a named human decision owner. Preparing a changed saved revision retains exact prior bundle/source history and creates a new pending review without reusing decision receipts; portable exports retain revision evidence. These are foundations for reversible contradiction review. No semantic contradiction is discovered, no real Book-owner review or accepted-knowledge change is performed, and no live source watcher automatically requests re-review. The authored cross-book outcome remains planned.

### MOONCHAT-R01

Status remains partial. R18: New generic authoring helpers bind explicit question/objection/proposal entries and human decision requirements to observed source SHA-256; supersession checks exact predecessor bytes and both observed source versions and leaves decisions empty. A real Town pending export passes the production native decoder/adapter and separate fixture decision outcomes. This is supporting contract and Town-producer evidence only. R01 specifically requires the selected host to open an exact MoonProj commitment or MoonFind artifact, so this Town extension does not close that criterion or qualify Proj/Find revision UX, keyboard access or real human review. Existing Find partial evidence and all remaining acceptance are preserved.

### Existing group next actions

- moontown-review: R18 completed locally: one-off collaboration-plan authoring with captured source snapshots, explicit participant/reviewer assignments, durable drafts and portable pending handoffs. Next execute and qualify the actual sourced synthesis/Book-owner decision-return path and semantic contradiction/re-review outcome; authoring briefs and fixture decisions are not accepted synthesis or real authority.
- moonchat-host: R18 adds generic source-bound bundle authoring/supersession and actual Town-producer adapter fixtures. ## Bounded round-eight authoring progress (r19, 2026-10-06)

R19 appends accepted round-eight Robo digital design, MoonMoon mission alternative authoring and Flow graph authoring through Desk evidence to eight existing requirements and six existing groups. It preserves all authored criteria, statuses, history, conditional estimates and the 20-product portfolio.

All 20 existing products, 344 requirements, 61 groups, authored criteria, statuses, history, scope and conditional estimates remain unchanged. Totals remain 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and one verified. No completion percentage or revised remaining estimate is inferred.

R19 qualification is bounded source/unit/UI-fixture and actual isolated native CLI/store execution. It is not mounted-browser/keyboard/accessibility, installed-Mac, live-provider, calibrated physics, external dataset truth, physical readiness or dispatch, customer outcome, publication or deployment acceptance. Robo is one generic box-link excavation-haul concept and its validator checks artifact consistency rather than executing scenarios. MoonMoon and Desk require manual native command steps; declared mission power assumptions remain inherited, and newly imported robot calibration is cleared. Desk/Flow source inputs are existence/containment checked but their contents are not frozen, so immutable versioned inputs are required. Test counts overlap and do not measure product completion.

### moonrobo

R19: The digital-design workbench edits the supported excavation-haul task loop, mechanism inventory, scenarios and speed limits, saves immutable named revisions, runs the existing profile/URDF builder and validator, reopens exact generated artifacts and exports a hash-bound robot-input transport consumed by MoonMoon. Exact retries preserve identities; stale drafts, partial builds and artifact changes refuse. Physical profile/runtime remain untouched. This is a bounded generic digital concept with structural validation, not general CAD, calibrated dynamics, scenario execution or physical readiness.

### moonmoon

R19: The study panel forks explicit route, energy, speed, seed and supported fault assumptions, optionally consumes actual Robo-built inputs, exports/reopens the runnable proposal, and imports real native simulator output as a new reference/edited comparison needing fresh review. Actual producer output, negative-energy/unknown-fault refusal and independent positive/negative mission variants were checked. Both alternatives use the newly imported robot, prior calibration/fault models are cleared and inherited power assumptions remain explicit. Manual export, native run-draft and reimport are required; there is no Run-in-UI endpoint or new physical authority.

### moonflow

R19: Versioned plan forms edit purpose, inputs, outputs, dependencies, explicit review and rerun declarations using the published capability catalog. The real CLI validates and prepares canonical graph/proposal bytes and imports or creates a child revision while retaining parent history. Actual Desk form output is byte-identical through CLI preparation. Invalid operations/graphs, repeated/stale actions and expired health remain blocked; tested plans create zero execution attempts. This expands authoring and revision preparation, not live adapter execution or permission.

### moondesk

R19: Desk now presents the Flow plan authoring form, round-trips drafts, preserves fields/unsaved work across transient refresh, fences stale callbacks and binds saved drafts to the selected workspace. Exact real-CLI graph preparation/import/revise is independently exercised after export. The documented manual CLI step remains required, and the existing execution transport stays deny-only. Live browser, ordinary-user/keyboard usability and full configured execution are not newly qualified.

### MOONROBO-B01

Status remains partial. R19: Immutable design revisions bind the supported profile, generic URDF, build/validation receipts and exported input bytes; reopened work rejects changed artifacts and stale draft digests. The actual package reaches MoonMoon with matching producer bytes. This supports digital identity only; real calibration, task/environment readiness and hardware revision invalidation remain separate acceptance.

### MOONROBO-B03

Status remains partial. R19: A cockpit design form invokes the existing native excavation-haul builder/validator and exports corrected immutable revisions with simulation-only labels. Geometry stays generic box-link and validation does not execute declared scenarios; the full supported digital-design acceptance remains partial.

### MOONMOON-B05

A new imported robot supplies both comparison alternatives and clears prior calibration/fault models. This is supporting producer-consumer identity evidence, not expanded physical authority or broad typed-design qualification.

### MOONMOON-B06

Status remains unverified. R19: Real native run-draft stages explicit edited inputs in a fresh workspace, runs existing simulation/replay/evaluation and returns a new source-bound dossier. Independent variants include accepted digital, short-route rejected and missing-coverage rejected results; negative energy and unsupported faults remain rejected. Manual run/reimport and inherited model assumptions remain explicit. No broad scientific calibration, external input truth or full authored receipt acceptance is inferred.

### MOONMOON-R01

Exact parent evidence is retained, prior robot results are marked incomparable, and new results require review rather than inheriting old decisions. ### MOONDESK-B05

R19: Desk's authoring form exports the actual plan that the native Flow CLI prepares/imports/revises byte-identically, retains purpose/input/output/review/rerun declarations, and exposes clear pending/manual steps. No mounted browser or live configured execution path is claimed; deny-only transport remains unchanged, so the complete published Work graph host outcome stays unverified.

## One-off participant work, result review and Book return (r25, 2026-10-07)

R25 appends accepted round-fourteen Town one-off participant execution, retained result, embedded Chat review and Book return evidence to twelve existing requirements and five existing groups. Claw's shared one-off runtime support is attributed here; Chat and Book production sources remain unchanged. Authored criteria, statuses, history, estimates and all 20 portfolio products are preserved.

All 20 existing products, 344 requirements, 61 groups, authored criteria, statuses, history, scope and conditional estimates remain unchanged. Totals remain 179 partial, 97 unverified, 67 planned and one verified. No completion percentage or revised remaining estimate is inferred.

R25 qualification is cloud source, compiled host/UI/controller fixtures, actual production Claw proposal/workflow/artifact persistence with a deterministic local fixture worker, and the unchanged production Book CLI/store. Reviewer contexts and participant text are synthetic fixtures, not real model or human acceptance. Town's own path creates no schedule/daemon/recurring goal and does not materialize or rewrite participant Book sources; configured runtime tools retain their existing policies and authority. Collected attributed outputs are not model-written synthesis or consensus. No live browser, full identity-service authentication, real provider, customer, hardware, Mac, push or deployment is qualified.

### moontown

R25: A prepared source-bound collaboration can run participants once through Claw, retain actual output/failure artifacts, reopen an attributed collected result and request each named owner's independent approve/reject/defer decision. Review binds produced result bytes, not the old briefs; earlier dissent and decision versions remain inspectable. Fully terminal accounts, including explicit partial failures, can be sent to a chosen Book only after every named owner approves their accuracy, whose independent disposition returns to the exact collaboration. Runtime readiness and operator identity remain existing setup requirements; partial/failed/uncertain work is visible and never silently replayed.

### moonclaw

R25: The shared accepted union adds an opt-in one-off proposal/runtime path with stable packet/profile/workspace binding, persisted proposal/run identity and an unstarted run before execution. Town uses the lower runtime API instead of recurring scenario installers. An unstarted Pending run may resume once; uncertain Running state is not automatically repeated. Existing ordinary proposal behavior and configured runtime authority remain distinct.

### moonchat

R25: Town consumes the unchanged embedded review contract against actual retained collaboration-result identity. Named owner decisions, independent objection ownership, append-only decision versions, deterministic reopening and receiver-independent Book return are exercised in production host/controller/native fixtures. Chat remains a contract, not a new runtime or standalone chat application; synthetic host identities are not real-human authentication.

### moonbook

R25: The unchanged product-outcome receiver now consumes Town's actual captured sources, produced participant outputs and exact Chat review dossier with explicit interpretation/gap statements. Idempotent import/readback reopens the original Town result, while Book owns separate deliverable and Three-Gap decisions. This is integration reuse, not new Book production behavior or accepted knowledge.

### Separate infrastructure outside the MoonSuite PRD portfolio

R26: LunaNexa's Benchmarks workbench keeps typed raw recorded observations, shows exact sample/model/runtime/hardware context, asks explicitly for genuinely absent provenance/source/workload/unit/memory metadata, and prepares the existing deterministic capacity packet without hand-authoring JSON. A native read-only benchmark-evidence exporter and file import converge on the same validator and produce byte-identical packets from the same input. Legacy summary-only telemetry remains readable with raw-history-unavailable disclosure and complete-evidence-file fallback. Synthetic and local evidence stay separate, historical rounded recommendation semantics are preserved, exact-count objective status remains authoritative for the new comparison, and no pooling/extrapolation/untested SLA claim is added. Drafts stay tab-local and require explicit export before reload/logout/close. LunaNexa remains separate infrastructure and acquires no MoonSuite dependency.
